# Agents · L · part 8

931 entries, lit-review-planner to loan-vs-invest-analyst. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lit-review-planner**: Systematic literature review protocol architect and evidence synthesis planner ([zivtech/joyus-desktop](https://github.com/zivtech/joyus-desktop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-review-planner.md))
- **lit-review-synthesizer**: Use to review, synthesize, or organize academic literature from the Zotero library or from several papers: summaries, comparisons of methods and identification strategies, intellectual lineages, gaps in the literature, comparison tables, and structured literature reviews in empirical macroeconomics and monetary economics. ([nreigl/dotfiles](https://github.com/nreigl/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/.claude/agents/lit-review-synthesizer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: Literature review specialist: systematic review (PRISMA 2020), OpenAlex/Semantic Scholar search, citation mapping, research gap identification, and bibliography management. ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/empirical-research-team/agents/lit-reviewer.md) · ★455 · 2 copies)
- **lit-reviewer**: 真实文献调研专用。给定一个生态学/行为学/演化生物学/人工生命的问题，负责实际检索（WebSearch/WebFetch）、筛选、精读，产出**可直接并入 docs/&lt;topic&gt;.md 的 [现实] 标签散文段落**，外加每篇的 notes/papers/&lt;citekey&gt;.md 笔记草稿与 lit/literature-log.md 台账行，每条引用附 DOI 或 arXiv ID。以下情况应当派给它：「自然界里 X 是怎样的」「这个设计有没有真实生物学依据」「piosphere / landscape of fear / Weismann barrier 的文献怎么说」「这个想法在 ALife 里有没有人做过」「给 docs/biology.md 补一节」「这条引用靠不靠谱」。它只读不写，返回的是待落盘的草稿，由派活的人写进文件。不要用它分析本地实验结果（result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/Alicization](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/Alicization/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: 文献调研专用。给定一个主题、方法名或研究问题，负责实际检索、筛选、精读并产出按主题聚类的结构化综述与研究 gap 列表。以下情况应当派给它：「这个方向已经有哪些工作」「XX 方法最近的进展」「我这个想法有没有人做过」「帮我找几个可比的 baseline」「补一下相关工作」「这篇论文的方法和我们的有什么区别」。不要用它来分析本地实验结果（那是 result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（那是 claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/TEOW](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/TEOW/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: Synthesize research notes into themes and argument skeleton (synthesis mode) or research gap map (gap mode). Dispatched by literature-review skill. Not user-invokable directly. ([Avishekdevnath/SRA-Smart-Research-Assistant](https://github.com/Avishekdevnath/SRA-Smart-Research-Assistant/blob/HEAD/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: 检索并核验文献，产出带出处定位与可信度分级的证据表、缺口矩阵和最近邻对比。用于需要 literature review、找研究缺口、确认某方向是否已被做过、或核对一组引用是否支持其主张时；只报告证据，不写代码、不改项目文件。 ([RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi](https://github.com/RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-scout**: Parallel literature and evidence scout that retrieves, classifies, and synthesizes sources using a structured evidence-quality taxonomy. Prioritizes empirical data over opinion; surfaces contradictions explicitly. ([sliamh11/Deus](https://github.com/sliamh11/Deus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★51)
- **lit-scout**: Literature evaluation specialist. Reviews PRE-READ paper subsets, extracts evidence claims with provenance (DOI + quote + section), identifies gaps and contradictions. Works with structured JSON, NOT raw PDFs. ([rhowardstone/Claude-Code-Scientist](https://github.com/rhowardstone/Claude-Code-Scientist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★7)
- **lit-scout**: 文献侦察。多模态并行检索同一问题，返回去重后的证据摘要而非文献列表。当需要了解某个主题的现有工作、查找阴性结果、追溯某个说法的原始出处、或在 KG 覆盖不足时替代图检索时使用。 ([HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow](https://github.com/HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★2)
- **lit-scout**: Finds and reads papers relevant to a stated question and writes verifiable notes to literature/papers/. ([Acceleratorer/quantum-circuit-compilation-clean](https://github.com/Acceleratorer/quantum-circuit-compilation-clean/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **lit-scout**: Local-first literature scout for /lit-position Step 1. Sweeps the local Zotero index for a research question's method and setting terms, follows citation chains inside the library, and returns a compact candidate table with a proximity guess and scooping flags. Never searches externally, never ingests, never writes. ([EconGeo/research-claude](https://github.com/EconGeo/research-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★1)
- **lit-scout**: Finds and summarizes papers for the thesis. Use for related-work searches, checking whether a method has been used before, or finding a citation for a claim. Search and summary only, no writing into the thesis. ([eunai9/llm-org-comm-thesis](https://github.com/eunai9/llm-org-comm-thesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **lit-scout**: 개선 정체(5회 연속 not_improved) 때 호출. 휴지 전압 기반 SOC/OCV 추정의 구현 가능한 아이디어 3개를 서로 다른 변경 유형으로 돌려준다. ([KingJeHyeok/BMS](https://github.com/KingJeHyeok/BMS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **lit-scout**: Finds and reads papers and industry sources relevant to a stated question and writes verifiable notes to research/lit/. ([M4-on-Github/Gemma4_Dev_Agent_Kaggle](https://github.com/M4-on-Github/Gemma4_Dev_Agent_Kaggle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **lit-scout**: Search the literature for papers relevant to a specific question and return a ranked shortlist with reasons, not a bibliography dump. Use when starting a new direction and you need to know what already exists, when a reviewer or advisor asks "has anyone done this", when looking for a baseline to compare against, or when you need the grounding citation for a design decision you already made. Runs in parallel — dispatch several with different framings of the same question rather than one broad query. Returns candidates for the main agent to triage; does NOT decide what to read or what to cite. ([mxzhuang/claude-skills](https://github.com/mxzhuang/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **lit-scout**: Busca e síntese de literatura científica e mineração de bases biológicas — PubMed, bioRxiv, Europe PMC, Open Targets, STRING, Reactome. Use para revisão bibliográfica, estado da arte, checagem de precedente, ou quando precisar sustentar uma afirmação com fonte primária. ([patoangolano/bio-stack](https://github.com/patoangolano/bio-stack/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/lit-scout.md) · 3 copies)
- **lit-scout**: Web and docs research for methods and library APIs — 3D synthetic-lesion techniques, PyRadiomics feature families, MONAI APIs, reconstruction-based anomaly-detection prior art. Isolated so reference-reading never floods the main context. ([rkg209/3D-MRI-Unsupervised-Anomaly-Detection](https://github.com/rkg209/3D-MRI-Unsupervised-Anomaly-Detection/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **lit-scout**: Systematic academic literature discovery with bibliography chaining. Use when the user needs to find scholarly papers, trace citation networks, discover datasets, or build a bibliography on a topic. Handles forward and backward citation chaining via CrossRef, Semantic Scholar, and OpenAlex. Produces a draft report with an explicit VERIFICATION PENDING marker; verification runs as a separate serial agent, started by the lit-scout command. ([saross/research-agent-toolkit](https://github.com/saross/research-agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/proposer-verifier/agents/lit-scout.md))
- **LIT-Scout**: Literature retrieval to saturation and adversarial novelty prosecution ([xuxiguo/research-factory-claude](https://github.com/xuxiguo/research-factory-claude/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/LIT-Scout.md))
- **lit-scout-verifier**: Adversarially verifies a lit-scout report for confabulation. Re-queries every cited DOI against the authoritative metadata API and flags any mismatch in authors, year, title, or citation count. Produces a corrections-applied audit trail and an integrated final report containing the original table, a verification block, and the corrected table. Started as a serial agent by the lit-scout command after the lit-scout proposer returns; can also be run on its own against any earlier lit-scout draft with the lit-scout-verify command. ([saross/research-agent-toolkit](https://github.com/saross/research-agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/proposer-verifier/agents/lit-scout-verifier.md))
- **lit-search**: 文獻搜尋代理，只做量大的機械工作：對一批起點文章做引用追蹤、核對書目、補摘要，交回可直接匯入文獻庫的批次檔。主對話需要對十幾篇種子文章做引用追蹤時使用；新主題的「發現」由主對話自己做，不交給這個代理。 ([nebula0/zotero-llm-wiki](https://github.com/nebula0/zotero-llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/plugins/zotero-llm-wiki/agents/lit-search.md) · ★1)
- **lit-triager**: You triage extracted full text into category-specific evidence summaries. ([matthiaskloft/claude-skills](https://github.com/matthiaskloft/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/scientific-literature/agents/lit-triager.md))

- **literature**: Proof Pursuit worker, the only one with web access. SOLVE mode (Phase 1L) — maps the literature for one cell, attempts it with known techniques, and compares with the blind results (divergence, never merged). ANALYST mode (Phase 3) — for a cell not solved, maps what is known, reduces the cell to sub-problems, attempts the reachable ones, and explains precisely why the cell can't be solved now. Requires a prepared run/tasks/&lt;task-id&gt;/ folder with brief.md. ([defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam](https://github.com/defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/literature.md) · ★1)

- **litmus-atomizer**: Phase 0 of Litmus. Reads an input document, classifies its type, and extracts every load-bearing claim as an atomic proposition tagged problem/solution/assumption/prediction/numeric. Output is JSON conforming to atoms-schema. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-atomizer.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-expert**: Specialist for Litmus Edge concepts, terminology, drivers, and API behavior, answered from the live docs.litmus.io resources rather than training data. Invoke when the user asks what something in Litmus is, how a Litmus feature or protocol works, which tool or SDK function covers an operation, or when the main session needs grounded Litmus context it does not already have. ([litmusautomation/litmus-mcp-plugin](https://github.com/litmusautomation/litmus-mcp-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/litmus-expert.md))
- **litmus-grounder**: Phase 2 of Litmus, the novel piece. For each atomic claim (especially numeric and prediction atoms), semantically search the web via Claude's inbuilt exa (mcp__claude_ai_Exa_2__*) and produce a citation table classifying the claim as GROUNDED / CONTRADICTED / UNGROUNDED / UNFALSIFIABLE. Uses the bundled Claude exa, not the user's local exa MCP. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-grounder.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-independent-designer**: Phase 1 of Litmus. Receives ONLY problem-statement atoms (NOT the source document) and produces a fresh design for the stated problem. The frame-break: the designer cannot anchor on a document it has never seen. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-independent-designer.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-accountability**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Accountability lens. Identifies who owns this in production, who gets paged, who has authority to roll back, chains of responsibility, oversight gaps, compliance/regulatory accountability, enforceability of stated guarantees (SLOs, SLAs, data-retention promises). Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-accountability.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-cascade**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Cascade lens. Second- and third-order effects, lock-in, vendor capture, copycat propagation (other teams will mimic this), maintenance debt compounding, cognitive-load tax, supply-chain blast radius, downstream-system impact. Time horizons: 0-6 months, 1-3 years, 5-10 years. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-cascade.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-escalation**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Escalation lens. Narrative of worsening failure over time, cracks → amplification → reckoning. Walks the timeline of how this fails (week 2, month 4, month 12, year 2), not the snapshot. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-escalation.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-integrity**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Integrity lens. Forensic audit of the document's foundational soundness, internal contradictions, evidence quality of the doc's own claims, logical gaps between problem and solution, assumptions stated as facts, numeric claims contradicted by the citation table. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-integrity.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-spectrum**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Spectrum lens. Enforced breadth across feasibility / ethics / governance / human-factor / operational-cost axes. Surfaces axes the document did not consider at all. Single-axis review is the failure this lens catches. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-spectrum.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-sycophancy-judge**: Phase 4 of Litmus. Scores each Phase 3 lens output on five behavioral sycophancy signals. Lenses with scores above the collapse threshold are re-prompted by the orchestrator. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-sycophancy-judge.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-synthesizer**: Phase 5 of Litmus. Reads all prior-phase artifacts and produces report.md per references/report-format.md. No verdict line; evidence-first. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **litrev-dataset**: Step-3 dataset-record worker for the reviewing-literature skill. Fills one or more dataset records from litrev dataset-info, the official homepage and cached paper texts, and writes data/datasets_step3/&lt;slug&gt;.json. Give it the prompt path and the inputs. ([orshij/reviewing-literature](https://github.com/orshij/reviewing-literature/blob/HEAD/plugins/reviewing-literature/agents/litrev-dataset.md) · 2 copies)
- **litrev-details**: Step-5 extractor for the reviewing-literature skill. Reads one paper's cached full text and writes data/details/&lt;P-id&gt;.json. Dispatched once per included paper; give it the method-details prompt path and the inputs. ([orshij/reviewing-literature](https://github.com/orshij/reviewing-literature/blob/HEAD/plugins/reviewing-literature/agents/litrev-details.md) · 2 copies)
- **litrev-reader**: Text-only worker for the reviewing-literature skill (step 0 survey taxonomy, step 6 clusters, explainers, winner critiques, member limitations, synthesis and limitations matrix, step 8 presentation plan and chapter scripts). Reads the cached text or packet named in its dispatch and writes one JSON file. Give it the prompt path and the inputs. ([orshij/reviewing-literature](https://github.com/orshij/reviewing-literature/blob/HEAD/plugins/reviewing-literature/agents/litrev-reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **litrev-screener**: Screening worker for the reviewing-literature skill (step 1 and refresh). Reads one or more screening packets (data/screening/input-NN.json), judges each paper against the review's scope with prompts/screen-papers.md, and writes one verdict file per packet. Give it the prompt path, the review and the packet paths. ([orshij/reviewing-literature](https://github.com/orshij/reviewing-literature/blob/HEAD/plugins/reviewing-literature/agents/litrev-screener.md) · 2 copies)
- **litreview-updater**: Use to incrementally refresh an EXISTING literature review (from a prior /litreview run) with newly published papers, instead of re-running the full review. Searches Scopus + Consensus for papers since the last run, validates them, judges whether they preempt the review's own gaps or hypotheses (deliberation + scholar-evaluation), and merges the result into a dated, track-changed copy (&lt;basename&gt;_up_YYYYMMDD.tex) with a CHANGELOG. Schedulable weekly/monthly (draft + REVIEW REQUIRED when unattended). ([LARi-UQAC/ResearchTools](https://github.com/LARi-UQAC/ResearchTools/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/litreview-updater.agent.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)

- **LLAMA_CPP**: The Generate nodes under `llama_cpp` run a GGUF model directly inside the ComfyUI process through `llama-cpp-python`. They are intended for stateless text or multimodal chat completion. The workflow-owned Runtime Session nodes instead launch a local `llama server` process and keep its model loaded until the session closes; they do not require `llama-cpp-python`. ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_DAEMON_RESTART_PLAN**: ComfyUI 설정의 **llama.cpp Daemon** 영역에 `Restart internal daemon` 버튼을 추가한다. 버튼은 이 ComfyUI 프로세스가 소유한 supervisor와 `llama server`만 재시작한다. 연결된 외부 llama.cpp 서버와 `Llama.cpp Connect Session`에는 영향을 주지 않는다. ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP_DAEMON_RESTART_PLAN.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_DECISION_SESSION_PLAN**: `makoto-decision`으로 지정된 답변 중 하나를 선택하고 각 답변의 점수 기반 확률을 출력한다. 기존 `[llama.cpp] Generate` 및 저장된 워크플로의 입력과 출력은 유지한다. 새 결정 노드는 현재 Session 소켓을 재사용하며, 질문은 `[llama.cpp] Build Question (Prefill)`에서 전용 데이터 소켓으로 공급한다. ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP_DECISION_SESSION_PLAN.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_DOWNLOAD_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN**: - ComfyUI 프로세스의 `PATH`에 `llama`가 없을 때 설정에 `Download llama.cpp` 버튼을 보여준다. 버튼을 누르면 **ComfyUI의 사용자 디렉터리** 아래 `__llama_cpp/artifacts/`에 이 실행 환경에 맞는 [b11146 릴리스](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases/tag/b11146)를 설치한다. 사용자 홈 디렉터리(`Path.home()`)를 쓰지 않는다. - 현재 미커밋 작업에는 `backend/llama_cpp/llama_cpp_runtime.py`의 상태 조회, PATH 탐색, 자동 실행, 로컬 전용 HTTP 경계와 `frontend/src/llama-cpp-runtime-settings.ts`의 설정 ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP_DOWNLOAD_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_SERVER_SESSION_DESIGN**: - Add `[llama.cpp] Connect Session` alongside the existing `[llama.cpp] Create Native Session`. Both feed the existing `session` socket on `[llama.cpp] Generate` and Unload Session. - The node has a server base URL, an available-models COMBO, and an editable `model` STRING. `model` is the saved execution value; selecting the COMBO copies its ID into `model`. A workflow can execute from the saved U ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP_SERVER_SESSION_DESIGN.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_VIDEO_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW**: 기준 문서는 JamePeng `llama-cpp-python`의 [Loading a Local Video With Generic MTMD](https://github.com/JamePeng/llama-cpp-python/blob/main/README.md#loading-a-local-video-with-generic-mtmd) 절이다. ([craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal](https://github.com/craftingmod/ComfyUI-llama-multimodal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LLAMA_CPP_VIDEO_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md) · ★2)
- **LLAMA_CPP_WORKFLOW_RUNTIME_SESSION_PLAN**: `[llama.cpp] Create Runtime Session`을 추가한다. 워크플로가 이 노드를 실행할 때만 로컬 `llama server`를 시작하고, 기존 `[llama.cpp] Generate`와 `[llama.cpp] Unload Session`을 사용해 생성 후 종료한다. `llama_cpp_python` 설치나 Internal runtime의 상시 활성화는 필요하지 않다.
